# Third-Party Notices

Ferriprint is AGPL-3.0-only; see LICENSE. This generated inventory covers every
registry package in the committed Cargo.lock, including build, development and
platform-specific dependencies. It is intentionally broader than the linked
runtime subset. License expressions and verbatim license/copyright texts below
come from the exact fetched Cargo packages and are checked against Cargo.lock.
Dependencies are fetched at build time; this is not a vendored source bundle.

PAPPL and pappl-retrofit are design references only; no code from them is bundled.
